# Changes and collation decisions

Counts are distinct material loci against each witness; a locus can count against both. Certainty-only and macro-only differences are excluded. Confidence counts count each logged locus once. For explicit unresolved alternatives, witness counts compare the preferred first reading; they do not assert that the alternative is certainly wrong.

## Consequential corrections

- 143_scan008, margin dimension clause: $\dim X=1$ / $\mathfrak{X}$ → $\dim\mathfrak X=1$. The short dim is followed by the looped X, an equals sign and a single upright digit 1.
- 143_scan008, margin noun following des: \illegible{1} / \ill{} → \unsure{traits}. There is a t-like high cross-stroke followed by very compressed low strokes; traits is a tentative word reading, not an established recovery. The overlap prevents secure identification of the individual middle letters.
- 143_scan008, margin final X(I) and suspension points: $X'(I)$\ldots / \ill{} → $\unsure{\overline{\mathfrak X}'(I)|\mathfrak X'(I)}$\unsure{.....|....}. A detached short horizontal stroke lies above the final X-like glyph, just under dessus. It may be a bar on X or a stroke belonging to the preceding word. The prime and parenthesized I are present. Two strong points and three diminishing aligned traces follow; five is preferred with four retained as a count alternative. The simpler X does not establish a different variable.
- 143_scan008, ambient subgroup after sous-groupe de: \mathcal G_{X'}(I) / \mathcal{G}_{\mathfrak{X}'} → \mathcal G_{\mathfrak X'}(I). At the right margin after the G and its X-prime subscript there is a parenthesized upright I. H omits this argument.
- 143_scan008, small varphi immediately before (17): \varphi_i / \varphi_i → \unsure{\varphi|\varphi_i}. The small graph has an upper loop and a long lower return whose right terminal rises into a short hook. I cannot isolate a detached dot or a separate upright to the right as an i. A reduced index might be merged into that terminal, so the proposed reading keeps both alternatives and does not assert a definite omission by the author.

The second is a rounded two-lobed extension-group glyph, left uncertain between calligraphic E and Sigma under the main reader's convention. Its crossed-out lower index retains the crossed looped X and a small prime. The obliterated superscript is over the initial Psi, not over Psi_j as the H note says; ext remains uncertain.

A conventional short arrowhead does not preserve that geometry. Between G and 1, the shaft has a small downward turn at its left start and an open two-tine return at its right end. The candidate draws those source strokes without assigning a semantic direction to the terminal fork. The isolated tiny black tap above the first shaft is not promoted to a legible label. Main confirms the long converging/retraced segments and the final open two-tine return. Draw these shapes without assigning a direction to the fork; first two links remain bare and the isolated tap is not a label.

\end{tikzcd}}. Four expressions form two offset columns: the upper-left and upper-right are joined by =, each has a separate vertical pair of equality strokes to the expression below it, and the lower pair are joined by =. C collapses the two columns into one gathered stack; H gives a horizontal equality chain. The two existing alias crops jointly support this arrangement.
